These structures implement ticket-based authentication with request/response headers (CephXRequestHeader, CephXResponseHeader) and support for rotating keys. The CephXTicketHandler and CephXTicketManager manage client-side ticket caching and lifecycle. The CephxClientHandler (src/auth/cephx/CephxClientHandler.h/cc) implements the AuthClientHandler interface and is responsible for client-side authentication, including building authorizers via the build_authorizer() method and handling responses. The CephxServiceHandler (src/auth/cephx/CephxServiceHandler.h/cc) implements the AuthServiceHandler interface for server-side authentication verification.
